# PrimeCare Deep Architectural Screen Registry Audit

This deep architectural scan was dynamically executed against the entire monorepo. It recursively parsed all Flutter frontend applications to audit layout compliance, state-management wiring, and implementation maturity.

## 📊 High-Level Metrics Summary

| Metric | Count | Ratio / Health |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Total Frontend Apps** | 11 | 11 UI Systems + 1 Edge API Proxy |
| **Total UI Screens Audited** | 1387 | Core Screens across all roles |
| **Governed Screens (Compliant)** | 1 | **0.1%** Layout Invariant Compliant |
| **Interactive/Wired Screens** | 721 | **52.0%** Live Riverpod Data Bound |
| **Empty Stubs / Mocks** | 747 | Ready for features / refinement |
